Our framework is embodiment-agnostic, and is instantiated in autonomous driving (AD), where assurance methods for traditional simulation are most mature. Applied to two driving WMs, the lower rungs reveal a reversal: the model that ranks higher on visual generation quality (L0) ranks lower on action-following (L1-L2), so visual fidelity does not predict the action-robustness a closed-loop verdict depends on.

</details>


### [106] [Manual, Joystick, or Haptic Control? An In Vitro Comparison of Navigation Strategies for Robotic Interventional Neuroradiology Procedures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07253)
*Benjamin Jackson,Nikola Fischer,Harry Robershaw,Xingyu Chen,S. H. Hadi Sadati,Yang Li,Jeremy Lynch,Nasr Abdelsalam,Jonathon Buwanabala,Matthew Benger,Sara Sciacca,Naga Kandasamy,Marco Mancuso-Marcello,Parthiban Balasundaram,Sahan Guruge,Neelan Das,Alejandro Granados,Kawal Rhode,Thomas C Booth*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文评估了四种神经介入手术控制模式（设备模拟控制器带/不带触觉反馈、摇杆、手动操作），发现手动操作最快但各模式均安全（血管壁力低于0.70N穿刺阈值）；设备模拟控制器优于摇杆，触觉反馈有潜力但无显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估神经介入放射学手术中机器人控制器界面的安全性，通过力传感平台在体外模型中模拟手术操作，比较不同控制模式对操作安全性和效率的影响。

Method: 开发了定制血管内机器人、设备模拟控制器和力传感神经血管体模，10名介入神经放射科医生（4名新手，6名专家）使用四种控制模式（带/不带触觉反馈的设备模拟控制器、摇杆、手动操作）进行模拟导航，评估导航时间、峰值血管壁力、错误导管插入和脱垂事件，并进行用户分析。

Result: 手动操作最快（平均47.7秒），其他模式显著较慢（p<0.001）；所有模式的血管壁力均低于0.70N穿刺阈值，均安全；摇杆比手动操作产生更多脱垂事件（1.56 vs 0.13；p=0.018）；专家比新手错误导管插入更少（0.25 vs 0.62；p=0.035）且施加的血管壁力更小（p<0.0005），触觉反馈增强了专家优势；用户认为触觉开/关模式的直观性相似，且均优于摇杆（p=0.033）。

Conclusion: 设备模拟控制器在多数指标上优于摇杆界面；触觉反馈显示出有希望但非显著性的性能优势。

Abstract: Objective: To evaluate robotic controller interfaces for interventional neuroradiology procedures in-vitro incorporating a force-sensing platform to assess safety. Methods: A custom endovascular robot, device-mimicking controller, and sensorized neurovascular phantom were developed. Ten interventional neuroradiologists (4 novices, 6 experts) performed simulated navigations using four control modalities: device-mimicking controllers with and without haptic feedback, joystick-based input, and manual navigation. Navigation time, peak vessel-wall forces, incorrect catheterisations, and prolapse events were assessed, alongside user analyses. Results: Manual navigation was fastest (mean 47.7 s) compared to haptic-on (248.7 s), haptic-off (314.7 s), and joystick (392.6 s) modalities (p<0.001). Regardless of controller type, vessel-wall forces were below the 0.70 N puncture threshold; therefore all modalities were considered safe. Joystick produced significantly more prolapse events than manual control (1.56 vs 0.13; p=0.018). Operator experience was relevant to performance: experts made fewer incorrect catheterisations than novices (0.25 vs 0.62; p=0.035) and applied less vessel-wall force (p<0.0005); these effects were sustained across controllers but accentuated when haptics were on. Users perceived haptic on and haptic off as similarly intuitive, and more intuitive than joystick (p=0.033). Conclusion: Device-mimicking robotic controllers outperform joystick interfaces on most metrics; haptic feedback shows promising but non-significant performance benefits.

</details>


### [107] [Programmable Synchronization Graphs for Adaptive and Fault-Tolerant Modular Miniature Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07281)
*Okan Kulekcioglu,Arqam Bin Ahmad,Ines Garcia,Filipe Serra Alves,Onur Ozcan,M. Selim Hanay*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可编程同步图框架，用于模块化微型机器人的步态协调。通过将每个执行器-传感器对表示为网络节点，利用图耦合实现同步，用符号化链接调控不同模块组间的相位关系。实验在最多9个模块的物理机器人集群上验证了该方法能产生类似奔跑和踱步的接触模式，且稀疏d-正则拓扑可保持同步并降低耦合负担。该方法还具备容错能力，并可通过上置信界算法在线学习目标相位状态，相比集中式领导者-跟随者控制，最差相位误差降低约三倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在计算、通信和可靠性受限的条件下，协调大量不完美的模块化微型机器人是一项核心挑战，现有方法依赖于特权领导者、固定步态模板或密集通信，难以满足可扩展需求。

Method: 提出可编程同步图框架：每个执行器-传感器对作为网络节点，通过固定的子图内链接实现同类执行器组同步，通过少量带符号的子图间链接编程不同组间的相位关系。采用d-正则稀疏拓扑替代全连接，并结合上置信界算法在线学习最优链接。

Result: 在最多9个模块的物理机器人集群上，图耦合驱动同步涌现，符号链接实现从同相到反相的相位调谐；五模块机器人产生类似奔跑和踱步的接触模式。稀疏d-正则拓扑在降低耦合负担的同时保持同步。增加图度数可提升容错能力。上置信界算法能学习目标相位状态；相比集中式控制，最差相位误差降低约三倍。

Conclusion: 可编程网络拓扑为模块化微型机器人的步态相位编程、在线自适应和鲁棒性提供了一种紧凑的控制层，避免了集中式控制中领导者失效的故障模式。

Abstract: Modular miniature robots could provide scalable function in constrained environments, but coordinating many imperfect modules remains difficult when computation, communication and reliability are limited. A central robotics challenge is to coordinate many actuator-sensor modules without assigning a privileged leader, prescribing a fixed gait template, or relying on dense communication. Here we introduce a programmable synchronization-graph framework for modular miniature robots in which each actuator-sensor pair is represented as a network node and locomotor coordination is encoded through graph coupling. Fixed intra-subgraph links synchronize heterogeneous actuator groups, whereas a small number of signed inter-subgraph links program phase relationships between groups. In physical robot collectives with up to nine modules, graph coupling drives the emergence of synchronization, signed links tune the phase difference from in-phase to out-of-phase motion, and floor experiments produce gallop-like and trot-like contact patterns in a five-module robot assembly. Replacing dense all-to-all coupling with sparse d-regular topologies preserves synchronization while reducing the coupling burden. The same graph representation also captures fault tolerance: increasing graph degree increases the number of module deactivations tolerated before desynchronization. Finally, an upper-confidence-bound edge-selection algorithm learns inter-subgraph links that drive the system toward target phase states. In a separate deactivation benchmark, the graph-based controller avoids the leader-specific failure mode observed in centralized leader-follower control and reduces worst-case phase error by about threefold. These results establish programmable network topology as a compact control layer for gait phase programming, online adaptation and robustness to unit loss in modular miniature robots.

</details>


### [108] [TouchWorld: A Predictive and Reactive Tactile Foundation Model for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07287)
*Jianyi Zhou,Feiyang Hong,Yunhao Li,Yicheng Zhao,Yongjue Cen,Zirui Liu,Jiakang Huang,Zirui Chen,Ruiyang Zhang,Weizhuo Zhu,Xuhua Song,Shuo Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TouchWorld是一个用于灵巧操作的预测-反应式触觉基础模型，采用分层策略分离视觉-语言子任务规划、触觉世界模型预测、视觉-触觉目标条件动作生成和高频触觉残差修正，在六项长时域、高接触灵巧操作任务中，干净环境下成功率65.0%，人为扰动下53.7%，分别超过最强基线15.7和18.5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧操作方法中，视觉和语言虽能提供语义与几何引导，但无法可靠揭示隐藏的接触状态（如力、滑动、接触稳定性）。触觉虽能感知这些物理线索，但多数策略将触觉作为低频观测流融入单一动作模型，导致慢速任务推理、动作生成与快速接触反馈耦合在同一循环中，难以兼顾语义泛化与局部接触适应。

Method: TouchWorld采用分层策略架构：高层规划层（High-Level Planning Layer）生成可执行子任务并预测触觉子目标；视觉-触觉目标条件策略（Visuo-Tactile Goal-Conditioned Policy）生成名义动作块；触觉条件修正策略（Tactile-Conditioned Refinement Policy）利用近期触觉和本体感知反馈进行在线残差修正。触觉既作为预测性接触参考又作为快速反馈信号。

Result: 在六项长时域、高接触灵巧操作任务中，TouchWorld在干净环境下成功率达65.0%，在人为扰动下达53.7%，分别超出最强基线15.7和18.5个百分点。

Conclusion: TouchWorld通过将触觉同时作为预测性接触参考和快速反馈信号，在保持视觉-语言-动作策略语义泛化能力的同时，显著提升了局部接触适应性，证明了分层解耦的预测-反应式触觉框架在处理复杂接触任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation in everyday environments requires both anticipation and reaction: a robot must predict how contact should evolve while rapidly correcting local errors caused by slip, misalignment, unstable grasping, or force mismatch. Vision and language provide semantic and geometric guidance, but they cannot reliably reveal hidden contact states such as force, slip, and contact stability. Although tactile sensing exposes these physical cues, most existing policies treat touch as a low-frequency observation stream within a monolithic action model, coupling slow task reasoning, action generation, and fast contact feedback in a single loop. We introduce TouchWorld, a predictive-and-reactive tactile foundation model for dexterous manipulation. TouchWorld uses a hierarchical policy that separates vision-language subtask planning, tactile world-model prediction, visuo-tactile goal-conditioned action generation, and high-frequency tactile residual refinement. A High-Level Planning Layer produces executable subtasks and predicts tactile subgoals; a Visuo-Tactile Goal-Conditioned Policy generates nominal action chunks; and a Tactile-Conditioned Refinement Policy performs online residual correction using recent tactile and proprioceptive feedback. By using touch as both a predictive contact reference and a fast feedback signal, TouchWorld preserves the semantic generalization of vision-language-action policies while improving local contact adaptation. Across six long-horizon and contact-rich dexterous manipulation tasks, TouchWorld achieves 65.0% success in the clean setting and 53.7% success under human perturbations, outperforming the strongest baseline by 15.7 and 18.5 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [109] [Multimodal Voice Activity Projection for Turn-Taking in Social Robots with Voice-Activity-Related Pretrained Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07294)
*Antonio Cano,Guillermo Pérez,Luis Merino,Randy Gomez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MM-VAP框架，将音频-only的VAP扩展为音视频同步输入，通过LoRA适配预训练骨干网络，引入语义一致性损失，在NoXi、NoXi+J和Haru EDR数据集上取得改进，适用于社交机器人调解场景中的话轮转换预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交机器人在人类交互中担任调解者角色时，需要预测对话动态而非仅仅对停顿做出反应，现有音频-only方法不足以捕捉多模态的对话轮换线索。

Method: 1) 扩展VAP框架支持同步音视频输入；2) 使用预训练的音视频骨干网络并通过LoRA微调；3) 独立说话人编码后引入跨说话人注意力建模关系动态；4) 引入语义一致性损失正则化256状态输出空间。

Result: 在NoXi和NoXi+J数据集上超越现有基线，特别是在某些话轮转换事件上提升明显；Haru EDR语料库上的评估进一步支持了该方法在调解型人机交互中的适用性。

Conclusion: 多模态融合与语义一致性约束能有效提升话轮转换预测性能，该方向对面向调解场景的人机交互具有良好前景。

Abstract: Turn-taking prediction is a key requirement for social robots involved in human-human interaction, particularly in mediator settings, where the robot must anticipate conversational dynamics rather than merely react to pauses. This work presents a Multimodal Voice Activity Projection (MM-VAP) framework that extends the original audio-only VAP formulation to synchronized audio-visual inputs while preserving its self-supervised future-projection objective. The proposed approach builds on pretrained audio-visual backbones originally optimized for speech-related tasks and adapts them through Low-Rank Adaptation to the multimodal turn-taking problem. After independent speaker encoding, an inter-speaker attention stage models the relational dynamics required to project future voice activity. In addition, a semantic consistency loss is introduced to regularize the 256-state output space according to higher-level dialogue activity patterns. Experiments on NoXi and NoXi+J showed improvements over the current baselines, particularly for some turn-taking events. Additional evaluation on the Haru EDR corpus further supported the suitability of this direction for mediation-oriented human-robot interaction.

</details>


### [110] [Towards Reliable Aerial Ground Vehicle Collaboration: An Integrated Planning and Autonomy Framework for Field Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07350)
*Md Safwan Mondal,Luca Russo,James D. Humann,James M. Dotterweich,Pranav Bhounsule*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度强化学习（DRL）的无人机-无人车（UAV-UGV）协同路由规划与自主执行框架，解决无人机在长时间任务中因续航限制而需要与UGV移动充电协同的难题。该方法联合优化无人机访问序列与会合点，并引入在线重规划器（RARP）应对环境不确定性，最终通过户外实验验证，包括基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的搜索救援场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机在长时间任务（如监控、巡检）中面临续航短的问题，需要高效访问多个空间分布的兴趣区域（AOI），而无人机与地面车辆（UGV）协同移动充电是一种有前景的解决方案，但引入了紧密耦合的协同路由问题（包括路径规划、能量管理、会合调度等），现有方法在规划与执行之间仍存在差距。

Method: 1) 将问题建模为能量约束的协同路由任务，使用深度强化学习（DRL）规划器联合优化无人机访问序列和与UGV的会合点；2) 提出标准化的两层YAML任务API，用于捕获环境状态和构建轻量级同步动作序列；3) 基于PX4/MAVSDK（无人机控制）和ROS 2/Nav2（UGV导航）构建完整自主堆栈；4) 提出轻量级在线重规划器（RARP），处理环境不确定性。

Result: DRL规划器在最小化总任务时间方面优于基线启发式方法；RARP将能量裕度违规率从83.33%降至20.00%；户外实验验证了系统在动态任务（包括基于VLM危险检测的搜索救援场景）中的鲁棒协同导航与适应性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的集成规划与自主框架有效弥合了协同UAV-UGV路由规划与实地执行之间的差距，通过DRL优化与在线重规划机制显著提升了系统对环境不确定性的鲁棒性，并通过完整的户外实验验证了其在真实部署场景中的可行性。

Abstract: Limited flight endurance significantly restricts the operational range of unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) in long duration missions such as surveillance and inspection, where multiple spatially distributed Areas of Interest (AOIs) must be visited. These tasks require efficient routing determining the sequence of visits which directly impacts mission time, energy consumption, and overall feasibility. Pairing UAVs with unmanned ground vehicles (UGVs) for mobile recharging offers a promising solution, but introduces a tightly coupled cooperative routing problem involving UAV route planning, UGV road constrained movement, energy management, and rendezvous scheduling under uncertainty. In this work, we present an integrated planning and autonomy framework for reliable field deployment. We formulate the problem as an energy constrained cooperative routing task and solve it using a Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) based planner that jointly optimizes the UAV visitation sequence and rendezvous locations with the UGV, outperforming baseline heuristics in minimizing total mission time. To bridge the gap between planning and execution, we introduce a standardized two layer YAML based mission API that captures environment states and structures lightweight, synchronized action sequences. This framework is supported by a complete autonomy stack using PX4/MAVSDK for UAV control and ROS 2/Nav2 for UGV navigation. Furthermore, we propose a lightweight Rendezvous Aware Replanner (RARP) that operates online to handle environmental uncertainties, reducing energy margin violations from 83.33% to 20.00%. The full system is validated through outdoor field experiments, demonstrating robust cooperative navigation and adaptability in dynamic tasks, including a search and rescue scenario with vision language model (VLM) based hazard detection

</details>


### [111] [HumAIN: Human-Aware Implicit Social Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07357)
*Daeun Song,Nhat Le,Jeffrey Chen,Mohammad Nazeri,Amirreza Payandeh,Rohan Chandra,Reuth Mirsky,Ross Mead,Ling Xiao,Xuesu Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HumAIN通过知识蒸馏将隐式社交线索（如骨骼关键点、步态等）融入机器人导航规划，使用Transformer教师模型学习多模态表征，再蒸馏为轻量学生模型实现实时部署，在轨迹预测指标上平均提升29.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有社交机器人导航往往忽略人体细微骨骼线索（如步态、朝向）所揭示的社交意图，且缺乏高效融合隐式社交信号到规划环路中的方法。

Method: 提出HumAIN框架：1）Transformer教师模型融合历史图像、骨骼关键点、机器人状态和目标等多模态输入，学习鲁棒的人类感知轨迹表征；2）通过知识蒸馏将教师知识迁移至轻量学生模型，优化轨迹重建和隐层特征对齐，使学生从最小输入中推断复杂社交动态。

Result: 在轨迹预测指标上相比SOTA基线平均提升29.8%，验证了隐式全身线索在资源受限平台上的有效性。

Conclusion: 利用隐式全身行为线索并通过知识蒸馏实现高效推理，能够让机器人在有限计算资源下实现类人的社交感知导航能力。

Abstract: Effective social robot navigation requires sensitivity to human behavior, often revealed through subtle skeletal cues like gait and orientation. We present Human-Aware Implicit Social Robot Navigation (HumAIN), a novel framework that fuses implicit social cues directly into the planning loop via knowledge distillation. We first employ a transformer-based teacher model that fuses rich multi-modal inputs, including historic images, skeletal keypoints, robot state, and a robot's target goal, to learn robust, human-aware representations for the robot's future trajectory planning. To enable real-time deployment, we then distill this knowledge into a lightweight student model. By optimizing for both trajectory reconstruction and latent feature alignment with the teacher, the student learns to infer complex social dynamics from minimal inputs. Bridging the prediction-planning gap with an efficient distilled architecture, our method enables robots to reason about human behavior in a manner that is adaptive, robust, and socially compliant. We validate HumAIN through extensive experiments, where it improves trajectory prediction metrics by an average of 29.8% across all metrics compared to state-of-the-art baselines. These results highlight the benefit of using implicit, whole-body cues to achieve human-like navigation awareness on resource-constrained platforms.

</details>


### [112] [Behavior Foundations for Quadruped Robots: ABot-C0 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07370)
*Xufeng Zhao,Fuzhi Yang,Jianhui Chen,Li Gao,Zhang Meng,Jie Gao,Yao Zheng,Wenyu Liu,Menglin Yang,Minqi Gu,Yaru Zhao,Honglin Han,Shihui Su,Zixiao Tang,Liu Liu,Mu Xu,Yang Cai,Wenbin Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ABot-C0，一个面向四足机器人的通用运动控制系统，通过构建可扩展的多源运动数据管道（包含16,074个物理可行的运动片段）、流匹配通用策略（首次展示四足运动跟踪的缩放定律）以及三阶段特权到感知的越野地形框架，实现了从单一功能演示到产品级行为智能的跨越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人运动控制面临动物运动数据稀缺和跨本体重映射脆弱性的根本障碍，现有方法缺乏可扩展且物理可行的运动语料库，亟需建立通用的运动控制系统。

Method: 1) 构建数据金字塔：通过条件视频生成合成、标注动捕、遥操作和人工设计生成16,074个物理可行运动片段；2) 训练流匹配通用策略，首次验证四足运动跟踪缩放定律；3) 采用三阶段特权到感知框架（含时序LiDAR记忆和地形预测监督）实现全地形鲁棒移动。

Result: 成功实现零样本跟踪未见运动，在城市自主导航和同伴式多模态交互实验中展现出超越单一功能demo的产品级行为智能水平。

Conclusion: ABot-C0通过数据-策略-部署三位一体的设计，为四足机器人提供了向通用行为智能发展的有效范式，验证了训练规模扩展与运动跟踪性能之间的正向缩放关系。

Abstract: In embodied intelligence systems, the motion controller serves as the critical bridge between semantic reasoning and physical execution. Humanoid control has progressed rapidly through large-scale human motion-capture data and motion-tracking paradigm. However, producing quadruped robots motion corpora with scalability and physical feasibility faces more fundamental obstacles: animal motion data is scarce, and cross-embodiment retargeting remains fragile. We present ABot-C0, a generalist motion-control system for quadruped robots that establishes three complementary behavior foundations: a scalable multi-source motion-data pipeline, robust policy learning across motion tracking, locomotion, and scene interaction, and a unified deployment stack for reliable real-world operation. Fundamentally, we construct a data pyramid through conditional video-generation synthesis, annotated motion capture, teleoperation and human design, producing 16,074 physically feasible motion clips as the data foundation for various motion learning demands. We then train a Flow-Matching generalist policy that demonstrates for the first time quadruped motion tracking scaling law that its performance improves consistently as training scales up, with zero-shot capability to track unseen motions. Then, we push a step further for robust all-terrain traversal locomotion by adopting a three-stage privileged-to-perceptive framework with temporal LiDAR memory and terrain-predictive supervision. Collectively, these components form a motion generalist that coordinates multi-policy execution, smooth behavior transitions, energy-efficient control, and safety mechanisms for real-world deployment. Extensive experiments on urban-terrain autonomous navigation and companion-style multimodal interaction demonstrate that quadruped robots move beyond single-function demos toward product-level behavioral intelligence.

</details>


### [113] [PLED-VINS: A Point-Line Event-Based Visual Inertial SLAM for Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07374)
*Seunghun Lee,Jihun Nam,Dong-Uk Seo,Hyun Myung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PLED-VINS，一种基于事件相机的单目视觉惯性SLAM框架，通过熵-召回率评分图和点线鲁棒BA实现动态环境下的可靠状态估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态环境中移动物体和快速运动导致视觉SLAM状态估计精度下降，现有事件相机SLAM框架大多假设静态场景，缺乏特征可靠性估计方法。

Method: 1) 提出熵-召回率评分图，基于事件时间统计特征表征点和线特征的时间可靠性；2) 通过统一点线鲁棒集束调整估计几何可靠性；3) 设计自适应加权策略融合时间与几何可靠性，包括针对线特征的运动条件可靠性建模。

Result: 在包含移动物体的动态序列上，PLED-VINS改进了状态估计精度。

Conclusion: 所提出的时间-几何可靠性融合策略有效抑制了动态场景中的不可靠观测，提升了事件相机SLAM在动态环境中的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Dynamic environments remain a fundamental challenge for visual SLAM, where unreliable observations from moving objects and rapid motion degrade state estimation accuracy. Although event cameras preserve fine-grained spatio-temporal information, most existing event-based SLAM frameworks still assume static scenes and lack approaches to estimate the reliability of features. To this end, we propose PLED-VINS, a monocular event camera-based visual-inertial SLAM framework that enables robust state estimation in dynamic environments. We propose an entropy-recency score map to characterize the temporal reliability of both point and line features based on event temporal statistics. Concurrently, geometric reliability is estimated via a unified point-line robust bundle adjustment. Building upon these, we design an adaptive weighting strategy that fuses temporal and geometric reliability, including motion-conditioned reliability modeling for line features, to suppress unreliable observations. Experimental results demonstrate that PLED-VINS improves state estimation on the evaluated dynamic sequences with moving objects.

</details>


### [114] [Communicative Efficiency of Single vs. Multi-Axis Robot Neck Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07390)
*Chapa Sirithunge,Haewon Jeong,Qinghua Guan,Fumiya Iida,Josie Hughes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出信息论框架量化机器人颈部运动作为通信信道的效率，发现双自由度运动达到信息峰值（5.26 bits），三自由度反而下降并增加能耗，即"形态信息瓶颈"现象，挑战了"解剖完整性提升机器人表现力"的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类头颈部非语言交流对社交信号至关重要，但机器人颈部形态如何将运动转化为通信信息尚不清楚，缺乏量化框架来指导机器人形态设计。

Method: 基于机器人颈部平台采集84个视频刺激（覆盖3个旋转自由度，变化幅度、加速度和频率），用香农熵量化像素变化信号的信息量并测量能耗，同时通过感知实验验证运动语义的解读清晰度。

Result: 信息量在双自由度达到峰值，三自由度反而下降且能耗上升；运动参数影响具有参数依赖性（部分叠加、部分非线性）；最优配置实现5.26 bits信息量且能耗具有竞争力；多轴运动降低人类感知清晰度。

Conclusion: 提出"形态信息瓶颈"概念，挑战了"更多自由度/更高解剖完整性提升表现力"的直觉，建立量化形态设计基础，为机器人尤其是人形机器人颈部设计提供指导。

Abstract: Nonverbal communication through head and neck movement is fundamental to human social signalling, yet how robotic neck morphology translates motion into communicative information remains poorly understood. We present an information-theoretic framework characterising robot neck movement as a communication channel, quantifying information transmitted and energy expended across varied configurations. Using a robotic neck platform, we recorded 84 video stimuli spanning three rotational degrees of freedom (DoF), varying amplitude, acceleration, and frequency, measuring Shannon entropy of pixel-change signals alongside energy consumption. A perceptual study validated communicative interpretations of each motion. While humans typically engage one axis per gesture, robots are unconstrained by biological architecture, motivating tests up to 3 DoF. Yet communicative information peaks at two DoF and decreases at three despite rising energy cost, a phenomenon we term the morphological information bottleneck. Motion parameter effects were parameter-dependent, some additive, others non-linear. We introduce the Motor Information Space, a framework mapping entropy against energy to expose communicative efficiency across morphologies, in which the optimal configuration achieves 5.26 bits at competitive energy cost. Perception data further confirm multi-axis movements reduce clarity. These findings challenge the assumption that anatomical completeness improves robotic expressiveness, establishing a quantitative basis for morphological design in robots, especially humanoids.

</details>


### [115] [Initiation Safety: A Missing Dimension in Generalist-Robot Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07420)
*Zhijin Meng,Francisco Cruz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出机器人安全领域缺失的"启动授权"问题，即机器人在执行难以撤销的社交行为（如问候、未经邀请的抓取、进入他人空间）前应获得许可，并通过PAS框架（探测-授权-执行）在人形机器人上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用机器人安全框架主要关注运动安全和对话安全，但忽略了"机器人是否应该执行首次难以撤销的社交行为"这一关键问题。当前系统往往将高参与度得分或高置信度VLA策略作为行动许可，但"看到一个人"并不等同于"获得被其招呼的同意"。

Method: 提出PAS（Probe-Authorize-Speak）框架，将启动授权作为独立安全层，区别于传统的VLA护栏。在人形机器人doorway场景上实现PAS，与直接启动（direct-init）进行对比，基于日志轨迹评估，并设计三条件用户研究。

Result: 通过对比实验表明，直接启动方式忽略了社会许可这一环节，而PAS框架能在执行社交行为前进行授权探测，减少未经同意的社交互动。研究还揭示了度量标准、治理机制以及启动授权与基础模型生成之间的边界等开放问题。

Conclusion: 启动授权应作为通用机器人安全的独立层，现有的后规划VLA护栏不足以解决社交行为中的同意问题。PAS框架提供了一种可行方案，但还需要进一步研究评估指标、治理框架以及启动授权与基础模型生成之间的界限。

Abstract: Safety for generalist robots is usually discussed in terms of motion or dialogue. We argue a third question is missing: should the robot take its first hard-to-undo social action at all, such as a greeting, an uninvited grasp, or stepping into someone's space? We call this initiation authorization. Current frameworks rarely treat it as a separate safety layer. Today's stacks often skip this step: a high engagement score or a confident VLA rollout is treated as permission to act. But seeing a person is not the same as having their consent to be addressed. We frame initiation authorization within generalist-robot safety and contrast it with post-plan VLA guardrails, implementing PAS (probe-authorize-speak) on a doorway humanoid, comparing it with direct-init on logged traces, and proposing a three-condition user study, with open questions on metrics, governance, and where initiation ends and foundation-model generation begins.

</details>


### [116] [Immersive Social Interaction with VR and LLM-Assisted Humanoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07430)
*Niraj Pudasaini,Geeta Chandra Raju Bethala,Pranav Doma,Anthony Tzes,Yi Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Apple Vision Pro的沉浸式人形机器人遥操作框架，融合语音控制 locomotion、VR操控和双向社交交互，新手用户经过短暂熟悉后即可成功操作，在物体操控和社交传递任务中分别达到80%和70%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥操作界面要么需要高强度的物理运动追踪，要么需要认知负担沉重的低级控制，缺乏一种既直观又易于上手的人形机器人远程操控方案。

Method: 利用Apple Vision Pro提供第一人称视觉反馈；LLM辅助的语音控制模块将自然语言指令转换为高层运动命令；操纵模块通过逆运动学和PD控制将人手运动重定向到机器人；同时记录多模态数据（RGB观测、语音/文本指令、关节状态、手部运动、眼动信号）。

Result: 新手用户在短暂熟悉后即可操作，物体操控成功率达80%，社交传递任务成功率达70%。

Conclusion: 沉浸式、语言辅助的遥操作可作为一种可访问的界面，用于人形机器人交互、远程协助和多模态数据采集，为未来的模仿学习和自主性研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Humanoid robots can extend human presence to remote, constrained, or hazardous environments, but existing teleoperation interfaces often require physically demanding motion tracking or cognitively demanding low-level control. This paper presents an immersive teleoperation framework that integrates voice-controlled locomotion, VR-based manipulation, and bidirectional social interaction for whole-body humanoid control. Using Apple Vision Pro, the operator receives egocentric visual feedback, issues natural-language locomotion commands, and teleoperates the robot's arms and dexterous hands through wrist and finger tracking. An LLM-assisted voice-control module converts spoken instructions into high-level locomotion commands, while the manipulation module retargets human hand motions to the robot through inverse kinematics and PD control. The system also records multimodal data, including egocentric RGB observations, voice/text commands, joint states, hand motions, and eye-gaze signals, supporting future imitation learning and autonomy. We evaluate the framework on a Unitree H1 humanoid equipped with dexterous hands in manipulation and social interaction tasks. Results show that novice users can successfully operate the system after brief familiarization, achieving 80\% success in object manipulation and 70\% success in a social cube-passing task. These results demonstrate the potential of immersive, language-assisted teleoperation as an accessible interface for humanoid interaction, remote assistance, and multimodal data collection.

</details>


### [117] [GeoGS-SLAM: Geometry-Only Gaussian Splatting for Dense Monocular SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07452)
*Lipu Zhou,Yaoyun Kang,Junxiang Pang,Shengkai Sun,Tingting Bao,Kehan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GeoGS-SLAM是一种仅关注几何重建的稠密视觉SLAM系统，通过仅保留空间参数将高斯基元参数减少80%以上，并提出局部平面驱动初始化和闭环地图更新策略，在几何重建质量和在线建图效率上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS框架同时建模外观和几何，但对SLAM任务而言，场景几何比外观渲染更关键（如导航、避障）。因此作者探究3DGS是否可以在不建模外观的情况下直接进行三维重建。

Method: 1) GeoGS仅保留空间参数，将每个基元的参数减少80%以上；2) 提出单视图和多视图几何与光度监督的训练框架；3) 提出局部平面驱动初始化，使基元更好地对齐局部结构；4) 提出闭环地图更新策略，全局变换高斯地图以对齐修正后的位姿估计，防止地图撕裂。

Result: 在合成和真实世界基准上，所提方法在在线建图效率和几何重建质量上均优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 仅几何的高斯泼溅表示在稠密SLAM中可行且高效，能减少基元数量、加速几何收敛、增强对光照变化的鲁棒性，并有效解决闭环中的地图一致性问题。

Abstract: Dense visual SLAM is a fundamental problem in robotics. Recent advances in 3DGS have demonstrated its potential for dense SLAM. Existing 3DGS frameworks focus on both appearance and geometry modeling. However, scene geometry is typically more critical for SLAM than novel view synthesis because downstream robotic tasks, such as navigation and obstacle avoidance, rely primarily on accurate spatial geometry rather than photorealistic rendering. This observation raises a natural question: Is it feasible for 3DGS to perform 3D reconstruction without scene appearance modeling? Motivated by this, we propose Geometry-only Gaussian Splatting (GeoGS), which directly reconstructs scene geometry, and further present GeoGS-SLAM, a dense visual SLAM system built upon this representation. Specifically, GeoGS retains only spatial parameters to reduce the number of per-primitive parameters by over 80%. In contrast to existing 3DGS methods, GeoGS focuses solely on geometric reconstruction, which significantly reduces the number of Gaussian primitives, accelerates geometric convergence, and enhances robustness to illumination variations. In addition, we present an effective training framework that optimizes the Gaussian primitives via single-view and multi-view geometric and photometric supervision, and speeds up geometry convergence with a local-plane driven initialization that better aligns primitives with local structures. Furthermore, we introduce a map update strategy for loop closure that globally transforms the Gaussian map to align it with the corrected pose estimates, thereby preventing map tearing caused by inconsistent per-viewpoint pose corrections in existing methods. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world benchmarks demonstrate that our method outperforms SOTA methods in terms of online mapping efficiency and geometric reconstruction quality.

</details>


### [118] [EmbodiedGen V2: An Agentic, Simulation-Ready 3D World Engine for Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07459)
*Xinjie Wang,Liu Liu,Taojun Ding,Andrew Choi,Chaodong Huang,Mengao Zhao,Ziang Li,Jackson Jiang,Chunlei Yu,Shengxiang Liu,Wei Xu,Zhizhong Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EmbodiedGen V2是一个生成式3D世界引擎，能够构建可直接用于具身智能策略训练和评估的可执行模拟环境。它通过统一的可模拟表示，连接跨模拟器资产、交互功能、任务驱动世界、大规模多房间场景和状态化Vibe Coding，形成一个生成式、可编辑、可复用的模拟管线。评估显示资产管线人工接受率96.5%、碰撞成功率98.6%，83.3%的任务驱动世界无需人工修改即可用于下游模拟。在线强化学习将模拟成功率从9.7%提升至79.8%，真实机器人任务成功率从21.7%提升至75.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前可模拟3D资产生成技术发展迅速，但将这些资产组装成可直接用于策略训练的任务环境仍然高度依赖人工，限制了具身智能领域的可扩展闭环学习。

Method: 提出统一的sim-ready表示，整合跨模拟器资产、交互功能、任务驱动世界、多房间场景和状态化Vibe Coding，构建端到端的生成、编辑、复用模拟管线。

Result: 资产管线人工接受率96.5%，碰撞成功率98.6%；83.3%的任务驱动世界无需人工修改；在线RL将模拟成功率从9.7%提升至79.8%，真实机器人任务成功率从21.7%提升至75.0%。

Conclusion: EmbodiedGen V2作为可扩展的模拟基础设施，有效支撑了具身策略的训练、评估和真实部署。

Abstract: We present EmbodiedGen V2, a generative 3D world engine for building executable sim-ready environments for embodied intelligence. Sim-ready 3D asset generation has advanced rapidly, yet assembling such assets into policy-ready task environments remains largely manual, limiting scalable closed-loop learning. EmbodiedGen V2 addresses this gap through a unified sim-ready representation that connects cross-simulator assets, interaction affordances, task-driven worlds, large-scale multi-room scenes, and stateful Vibe Coding into a generative, editable, and reusable simulation pipeline. The generated environments support manipulation, navigation, mobile manipulation, cross-simulator deployment, and embodied policy training. In evaluation, the asset pipeline achieves 96.5% human acceptance and 98.6% collision success, and 83.3% of task-driven worlds are directly usable for downstream simulation without manual modification. Online reinforcement learning with generated environments further improves simulation success from 9.7% to 79.8%, and transfers to real robots with task success increasing from 21.7% to 75.0%. These results establish EmbodiedGen V2 as scalable simulation infrastructure for training, evaluating, and deploying embodied policies.

</details>


### [119] [Agent-Exploitation Affordances: From Basic to Complex Representation Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07475)
*Bastien Dussard,Aurélie Clodic,Guillaume Sarthou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了合作可供性（cooperative affordance）的概念，并构建了可计算的 ontological 表示，使智能体能够在多机器人社会场景中理解并利用与其他智能体的协同行为来扩展自身的动作可能性范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有功能可供性研究聚焦于个体与工具/物体的交互，忽略了社会情境中多智能体共存时产生的互补性动作可能性。本文旨在填补这一空白，探索社会机器人中智能体如何通过合作来扩展动作能力。

Method: 从合作可供性的定义出发，提出了一种可计算的（tractable）本体论表示，并基于基本模式进行组合，以描述多样化场景。

Result: 构建了合作可供性的形式化表示，并通过组合基本模式验证了其在多种场景下的有效性与表达能力。

Conclusion: 合作可供性的本体表示能够使人工智能体理解并利用社会交互中的协同动作可能性，为社会机器人中的多智能体协作提供了知识表征基础。

Abstract: In robotics, the capability of an artificial agent to represent the range of its action possibilities, i.e. affordances, is crucial to understand how it can act on its environment. While functional affordances, which refer to the use of tools and objects, have been broadly studied in knowledge representation, the implications of a social context and the presence of other agents have remained unexplored in this field. Consequently, in the field of social robotics, a multi-agent context enables the agents to engage in new actions that are potentially complementary to their individual capabilities, leading to the perspective of agentexploitation. This work focuses on the concept of cooperative affordance within the realm of social affordances. Cooperative affordances refer to situations where agents interact with each other to extend their action possibilities range. From this definition, this paper proposes a tractable ontological representation of this concept with the aim of making it usable by an artificial agent. Expanding on those elementary patterns, we illustrate the effectiveness of these representations by combining them to depict a diverse range of scenarios.

</details>


### [120] [Smooth Operator: A Real-Time Sampling-Based Algorithm for Kinematic Hand Retargeting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07491)
*Robert Jomar Malate,Erik Bauer,Norica Bacuieti,Stefanos Charalambous,Elvis Nava,Robert K. Katzschmann,Benedek Forrai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于采样的无梯度重定向方法SBR，用于减少机器人遥操作数据中的抖动问题，在模拟和真实用户研究中均优于传统梯度方法，成功率达54.1%，NASA-TLX工作负荷评分36.4。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于梯度的重定向算法容易收敛到不同的局部最小值，导致抖动问题，影响遥操作数据质量和用户体验，进而限制了VLA和VAM等学习方法的性能上限。

Method: 提出了采样式重定向器(SBR)，一种无梯度的重定向方法，源于采样控制理论，专为低抖动、实时的运动学重定向设计。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界用户研究（18名参与者，3项复杂操作任务）中，SBR取得了最高的整体任务成功率(54.1%)和最低的NASA-TLX工作负荷评分(36.4/100)，显著降低了操作者的认知疲劳。

Conclusion: SBR被确立为一种高效、直观的灵巧操作重定向方法，并为社区提供了严格的基准测试方法来指导未来的重定向研究。

Abstract: Advances in learning-based robotic manipulation, such as Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models and Video Action Models (VAMs), heavily rely on high-quality teleoperation data. Their capabilities are strictly upper-bounded by the quality of the underlying human demonstrations. Current gradient-based retargeting algorithms often converge to different local minima, resulting in jitter that affects data quality and teleoperation experience. To address this, we introduce the Sampling-Based Retargeter (SBR), a novel gradient-free retargeting method drawn from the rich literature of sampling-based control and explicitly designed for low-jitter, real-time kinematic retargeting. We evaluate SBR both in simulation and through a rigorous real-world user study involving 18 participants performing 3 complex manipulation tasks. Compared to gradient-based baselines, SBR achieved the highest overall task success rate (54.1%) while significantly reducing operator cognitive fatigue, recording the lowest NASA-TLX workload score (36.4 out of 100). Ultimately, we establish SBR as a highly effective, intuitive retargeter for dexterous manipulation, providing the community with a rigorous benchmarking methodology to guide future retargeting research.

</details>


### [121] [Generating Personalized Lower-Limb Kinematics Across Walking Speeds Using Subject-Conditioned Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07533)
*Diya Dinesh,Adrian Krieger,Changseob Song,Dongho Park,Aaron J. Young,Inseung Kang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于主题条件残差扩散的框架，能够从单个已知速度下的步态序列生成个性化下肢运动学数据（涵盖髋、膝、踝关节），从而大幅降低外骨骼个性化所需的数据采集负担。该方法在健全受试者上达到3.4°的平均绝对误差，在未经微调的中风患者上达到6.0°，相较前馈基线降低了70%以上的误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外骨骼辅助个性化需要用户在不同行走任务下的步态数据，但传统的运动捕捉采集成本高、耗时长，尤其对临床人群负担沉重。这一问题在跨行走速度时尤为突出，因为步态变化显著且临床步态偏差更大。因此，亟需一种能从小样本数据中合成个性化步态的方法。

Method: 提出主题条件残差扩散框架：给定受试者在某个已知速度下的矢状面髋、膝、踝关节轨迹及目标未知速度，模型生成残差将已知轨迹变换为目标轨迹。采用Transformer去噪器，通过特征线性调制（FiLM）以受试者步态和两个速度条件作为条件。模型仅在健全数据上训练。

Result: 在健全受试者上MAE为3.4°；在未做任何中风特异性微调的中风患者上MAE为6.0°，保留了受试者身份特征。相比有监督前馈基线，误差降低70%以上；单已知速度与四个已知速度的精度差距仅0.4°。

Conclusion: 主题条件残差扩散框架能够从极少数据中合成跨速度的个性化步态，显著降低了下游外骨骼个性化所需的数据采集负担。

Abstract: Personalizing exoskeleton assistance requires user-specific gait data across many locomotor tasks, yet collecting this data demands repeated motion capture sessions that are costly, time-intensive, and especially burdensome for clinical populations. This challenge is most acute across walking speeds, where gait changes substantially and deviates further in clinical gait. This work introduces a subject-conditioned residual diffusion framework that generates personalized lower-limb kinematics at unseen walking speeds from a subject's gait sequence at a single seen speed. Given sagittal-plane hip, knee, and ankle trajectories at a seen speed and a desired unseen speed, the model generates a residual that transforms the seen trajectory into the unseen one, using a transformer denoiser conditioned on the subject's gait and the two speeds through feature-wise linear modulation. Trained only on able-bodied data, the model achieved a mean absolute error (MAE) of 3.4° on held-out able-bodied subjects. Without any stroke-specific fine-tuning, it achieved a 6.0° MAE on out-of-training-distribution stroke subjects, retaining subject identity for clinical gait. The framework reduced the MAE by over 70% relative to supervised feed-forward baselines, and a single seen speed matched the accuracy of four speeds within 0.4°. These results demonstrate that subject-conditioned residual diffusion can synthesize personalized gait across speeds from minimal data, reducing the collection burden for downstream exoskeleton personalization.

</details>


### [122] [SonoRank: Towards Calibration-Free Real-Time Finger Flexion Detection from Forearm Ultrasound Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07542)
*Dean Zadok,Alon Wolf,Alex M. Bronstein,Oren Salzman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SonoRank，通过成对排序超声序列的相对运动幅度作为预训练信号，实现无需用户校准的手指屈曲检测，在12名受试者数据上F1分数比直接分类基线提升28%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有肌电假肢手依赖sEMG功能有限，超声虽能实时观察肌肉活动且控制自由度更高，但现有方法需要逐用户微调，限制了商业化推广。

Method: SonoRank首先学习对五根手指的超声序列按相对运动幅度进行成对排序，然后基于操作开始时采集的静息参考进行微调，分类每个手指是否主动屈曲。

Result: 在12名受试者的12折留一法交叉验证中，SonoRank的F1分数比跳过排序阶段的直接分类基线提升了28%。

Conclusion: 成对排序可作为有效的预训练信号实现受试者无关的控制，推动超声假肢向实际、免校准的部署迈进。

Abstract: Powered prosthetic hands are frequently abandoned, largely due to the limited functionality of current devices that rely on surface electromyography (sEMG). Sonomyography (ultrasound) has emerged as a promising alternative, owing to its ability to observe muscle activity in real time and control a greater number of degrees of freedom. Yet, existing ultrasound-based methods require per-user fine-tuning, limiting their commercialization. We propose SonoRank, an important step towards calibration-free finger flexion detection from forearm ultrasound video. SonoRank first learns to rank pairs of ultrasound sequences by their relative motion magnitude for each of the five fingers. The learned representations are then fine-tuned to classify whether each finger is actively flexing, using a rest reference that is captured at the beginning of the operation. Under 12-fold leave-one-subject-out cross-validation on a dataset of twelve subjects with synchronized kinematics, SonoRank achieves a 28% improvement in F1 score over direct classification baselines that skip the ranking stage. These results establish pairwise ranking as an effective pretraining signal for subject-independent control, bringing ultrasound-based prosthetics closer to practical, calibration-free deployment.

</details>


### [123] [Context-Aware Force Estimation for Deformable Tool Manipulation in Robotic Environmental Swabbing via Few-Shot Continual Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07574)
*Siavash Mahmoudi,Chaitainya Kuppar Reddy,Yang Tian,Dongyi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种数据驱动框架，利用本体感受和长短时记忆网络（LSTM）估计可变形拭子工具在机器人表面采样中的接触力，并通过参数隔离的少样本自适应策略（FiLM）泛化到未知表面和工具刚度条件，实验在UR5e平台上将零样本估计误差降低63%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人表面拭子采样需要精准的末端接触力估计以保证采样一致性，但可变形工具的粘弹性迟滞效应使腕部力传感器测量值偏离真实接触力，而工具端集成传感器因无菌性和一次性使用约束难以实际部署。

Method: 1) 通过时序模型比较实验选出紧凑LSTM作为骨干网络；2) 提出参数隔离的少样本自适应策略，冻结训练好的LSTM骨干，利用特征级线性调制（FiLM）引入低维上下文嵌入，仅学习少量领域特定参数以适应未知表面和工具刚度。

Result: 在UR5e平台上跨越九种工具-表面交互场景的实验表明：零样本估计误差最多降低63%，基线性能保持完好，无灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: 分离共享的变形-历史动力学与领域特定条件化，能够在非平稳环境中实现可变形工具操作（DTM）的可靠力估计。

Abstract: Robotic surface swabbing requires sustained interaction between a compliant tool and heterogeneous environments, where accurate estimation of tip-level contact force is critical for consistent sampling performance. However, deformable tool dynamics introduce nonlinear viscoelastic hysteresis that decouples wrist-mounted force measurements from true contact forces, while tool-integrated sensors are impractical for deployment due to sterility and disposability constraints. This paper presents a data-driven framework for contact force estimation in Deformable Tool Manipulation (DTM) that leverages proprioceptive sensing without requiring explicit physical models or permanent embedded sensing hardware at the tool tip. A recurrent architecture is first identified through a comparative evaluation of temporal models, where a compact LSTM achieves the lowest estimation error and sub-millisecond inference latency. To address generalization across unseen surfaces and tool compliance conditions, we introduce a parameter-isolated few-shot adaptation strategy that augments a frozen recurrent backbone with low-dimensional context embeddings using feature-wise linear modulation (FiLM). Experiments on a UR5e platform across nine tool-surface interaction regimes demonstrate that the proposed approach significantly improves robustness under domain shift, reducing zero-shot estimation error by up to 63\% while preserving baseline performance without catastrophic forgetting. These results show that separating shared deformation-history dynamics from domain-specific conditioning enables reliable force estimation for DTM in non-stationary environments.

</details>


### [124] [CARLA-GS: Decoupling Representation, Reasoning, and Physics Simulation for Autonomous Driving Corner-Case Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07601)
*Kaicong Huang,Meng Ma,Ruimin Ke*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CARLA-GS是一个模块化的自动驾驶边缘场景合成框架，通过解耦视觉表示（高斯泼溅场景）、语义推理（多智能体LLM）和物理执行（CARLA+PID控制器），实现从真实数据重建可编辑场景、生成意图级轨迹并渲染照片级真实视频，在Waymo数据集上验证了可控性、时空一致性和物理可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶安全评估需要罕见、安全关键的交互场景，现有方法要么孤立处理场景或轨迹组件，要么端到端扩散方法难以保证时空一致性和物理真实性，缺乏统一框架整合视觉表示、场景推理和轨迹生成与控制。

Method: 1) 从真实驾驶数据重建带几何一致性约束的可编辑高斯场景；2) 多智能体LLM进行场景级推理识别风险交互并生成意图级waypoint轨迹；3) CARLA中PID控制器确保运动学与动力学可行性；4) 将模拟车辆状态重投影回高斯场景进行自中心渲染。

Result: 在Waymo Open Dataset上定量和定性实验表明，该框架能够实现可控的边缘场景生成，产生照片级真实、时空一致且符合语义意图和物理可行运动的视频。

Conclusion: 通过解耦视觉表示、语义推理和物理执行并在模块间保持紧耦合，CARLA-GS统一了这些方面，实现了高层语义推理、低层物理可执行运动与照片级边缘场景生成。

Abstract: Safety evaluation for autonomous driving is dominated by rare, safety-critical interactions, motivating simulators that can deliberately synthesize corner cases with photorealistic observations. Corner-case generation is inherently a multi-source problem spanning visual representation, scene reasoning, and vehicle trajectory generation and control. Prior knowledge- and model-based approaches typically focus on scene or trajectory components in isolation, while diffusion-based methods attempt end-to-end generation but still struggle to ensure spatiotemporal consistency and physical realism. To unify these aspects within a single framework, we propose CARLA-GS, a modular corner-case synthesis pipeline that decouples visual representation, semantic reasoning, and physics-based execution while maintaining tight cross-module coupling. Starting from real driving data, we reconstruct an editable gaussian scene with additional geometry-consistent constraints. A multi-agent LLM then performs scene-level reasoning to identify risky interactions and generate intent-level waypoint trajectories, while the low-level motion control is delegated to CARLA, where a PID controller ensures kinematic and dynamic feasibility. The simulated vehicle states are finally re-projected into the gaussian scene for ego-centric rendering. This design enables high-level semantic reasoning, low-level physically executable motion, and photorealistic corner-case generation within a unified pipeline. Experiments on the Waymo Open Dataset show, both quantitatively and qualitatively, that our framework enables controllable corner-case generation and produces photorealistic, spatiotemporally consistent videos aligned with semantic intent and physically feasible motion.

</details>


### [125] [Dual Latent Memory in Vision-Language-Action Models for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07608)
*Hongyu Qu,Jianzhe Gao,Xiaobin Hu,Shaohuan Yang,Xinlei Yu,Rui Yan,Wenguan Wang,Xiangbo Shu,Shuicheng Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LaMem-VLA提出了一种将历史经验重构为潜在记忆令牌并与VLA推理直接交织的框架，通过在相同连续潜在空间中表示、检索和消费历史经验，使记忆直接参与VLA推理并在有限上下文下指导动作生成，在SimplerEnv和LIBERO上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强型VLA模型将记忆置于VLA推理的原生潜在嵌入空间之外，使历史经验无法与多模态推理和动作形成流畅交织，导致在长期、时间依赖任务中表现不佳。

Method: 提出LaMem-VLA框架，包含四个协同组件：curator（组织历史经验为短期和长期记忆库）、seeker（利用多模态认知从记忆库检索上下文相关证据）、condenser（将检索证据重构为紧凑的短期和长期潜在记忆令牌）、weaver（将记忆令牌与当前观测和指令注入为连续的嵌入序列）。

Result: 在SimplerEnv和LIBERO基准上的大量实验表明LaMem-VLA具有优越性。

Conclusion: 通过将历史经验完全在相同的连续潜在空间中进行表示、检索和消费，LaMem-VLA使记忆能够直接参与VLA推理并在有限上下文下指导动作生成。

Abstract: Mainstream Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models predict actions primarily from the current observation under a Markovian assumption, thus struggling with long-horizon, temporally dependent tasks. Existing memory-augmented VLAs either expand the observation window or retrieve history from the memory bank as auxiliary policy-side context. However, they leave memory outside the native latent embedding space of VLA reasoning, preventing historical experience from being fluidly interleaved with multimodal reasoning and action formation. To this end, we introduce LaMem-VLA, a latent-memory-native framework that reconstructs historical experience into latent memory tokens and directly interweaves them with VLA reasoning. At its core, LaMem-VLA introduces four coordinated components: (i) a curator that organizes historical experience into two complementary short-term and long-term memory vaults; (ii) a seeker that queries both vaults using the multimodal cognition to retrieve context-relevant evidence; (iii) a condenser that reconstructs the retrieved evidence into compact short-term and long-term latent memory tokens; and (iv) a weaver that injects these memory tokens with the current observation and instruction into one continuous embedding sequence. By representing, retrieving, and consuming historical experience entirely in the same continuous latent space, LaMem-VLA enables memory to directly participate in VLA reasoning and guide action generation under a bounded context. Extensive experiments on SimplerEnv and LIBERO demonstrate the superiority of our LaMem-VLA.

</details>


### [126] [Continuous and large-scale: ELEANOR, the soft architected arm inspired by the elephant trunk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07622)
*Giovanna A. Naselli,Anderson B. Nardin,Seonggun Joe,Ryan Drinkwater,Enrico Donato,Diego Bianchi,Egidio Falotico,Michel C. Milinkovitch,Lucia Beccai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于非洲象鼻仿生设计的连续体机械臂，通过3D打印制造了85厘米长、锥形、柔性且体积分层的结构，结合肌腱驱动模仿象鼻的纵向和斜向肌肉，实现了类象鼻的整体抓取运动，并在生物学与机器人学方面进行了对比讨论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大象鼻子的灵巧性和多功能性在机器人领域仍未被超越；现有工作多侧重于模块化和小型化连续体机器人，缺乏对结构连续性和动态特性的仿生模拟。

Method: 以非洲象鼻为生物模型，采用3D打印制造85厘米长的锥形柔性连续体臂，通过体积分层（volumetrically tessellated）设计实现结构连续性，并利用肌腱驱动机制模拟象鼻的纵向和斜向肌肉系统。

Result: 成功实现了对不同形状和尺寸物体的整体抓取（whole-body grasping），展示了类似象鼻的运动能力。

Conclusion: 基于自然系统宏观特性的仿生设计能够有效复现象鼻级的运动与抓取能力，为连续体机器人提供了无需预设行为的高适应性设计范式。

Abstract: The elephant trunk is a dexterous and versatile manipulator whose performance is still unmatched in robotics. In previous works, modularity was prioritized and relatively small-scale continuum robots were built. We take the natural proboscis of the *Loxodonta africana* species as a model and propose a different design approach which favors structural continuity and dynamic properties that plausibly emulate those of the natural trunk, while conferring high adaptability to the environment and humans. Instead of targeting prescribed behaviors, we show that a biomimetic design based on the macroscopic properties of the natural system enables elephant-like movements and grasping. We build by 3D printing an 85 cm long, compliant, tapered, volumetrically tessellated continuum arm, which is combined with tendon-driven actuation mimicking the longitudinal and oblique muscles of the natural model. We demonstrate whole-body grasping of objects having different shapes and dimensions and discuss a comparison to the biological trunk highlighting aspects of both biology and robotics.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [AgentLens: Production-Assessed Trajectory Reviews for Coding Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06624)
*Andrey Podivilov,Vadim Lomshakov,Sergey Savin,Matvei Startsev,Roman Pozharskiy,Maksim Parshin,Sergey Nikolenko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentLens是一个面向交互式代码智能体的生产级评估基准，它不仅评估任务是否完成，还通过形式化验证和LLM编写的轨迹评估来全面分析智能体在指令遵循、工具使用、错误恢复等方面的完整表现，并已用于模型诊断、版本对比和产品回归检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码智能体评估基准过于简化（仅以任务是否通过作为单一指标），忽略了用户实际使用中关注的完整执行轨迹，包括智能体如何遵循指令、使用工具、验证工作、从错误中恢复以及与人交互等关键行为。

Method: AgentLens采用双重评估机制：一方面通过形式化验证提供客观检查；另一方面引入LLM编写的轨迹评估和并排对比，为每次运行生成可读的评分解释。该基准已集成到产品夜间评估管线中用于模型诊断、版本对比和回归检测。

Result: AgentLens不仅能够对模型进行排名，还能用于诊断模型行为、比较智能体不同版本之间的差异，并在夜间评估管线中捕获产品回归问题。该基准已作为开源项目发布。

Conclusion: AgentLens通过结合形式化验证和LLM驱动的轨迹评估，提供了一种比传统单一指标更全面、更具可解释性的代码智能体评估方案，具有实际产品落地价值。

Abstract: We present AgentLens, a production-assessed benchmark for interactive code agents. Most code-agent benchmarks reduce a run to a single bit -- did the task pass? -- but the people who actually use these agents experience the entire trajectory: how the agent follows instructions, uses its tools, verifies its own work, recovers from mistakes, and talks to them along the way. AgentLens evaluates that whole trajectory. It pairs formal verification, where an objective check exists, with LLM-written trajectory reviews and side-by-side comparisons, so that each run yields a readable explanation of why the score is what it is. This makes AgentLens useful for more than ranking models: we use it to diagnose model behavior, compare successive versions of our own agent, and catch product regressions in a nightly evaluation pipeline. We release the benchmark as open source at https://github.com/agent-lens/agent-lens-bench.

</details>


### [128] [When Does In-Context Search Help? A Sampling-Complexity Theory of Reflection-Driven Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06720)
*Yotam Wolf,Noam Wies,Amnon Shashua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对大型语言模型的上下文搜索（in-context search）进行理论分析，将其建模为推理轨迹上的近似推断，证明当反思能可靠定位早期错误时，上下文搜索可指数级提升基础模型性能，且这种增益具有鲁棒性和可学习性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解LLM通过迭代生成、批判和修正解决方案（即上下文搜索）实现扩展推理的过程提供理论基础，分析其推理时采样复杂度及性能提升的根本机制。

Method: 将上下文搜索建模为推理轨迹上的近似推断：基础模型定义先验，自我反思提供反馈用于后验更新。通过理论分析研究推理时采样复杂度，并结合强化学习中的可验证奖励，证明最优策略扩展实现相同的后验重加权规则。最后在真实大推理模型上验证理论预测。

Result: 当反思能可靠定位早期错误时，上下文搜索可指数级提升性能（用多项式次顺序尝试解决零样本通过率指数级小的问题）；当不满足该条件时，条件化过去尝试相比并行采样无渐进优势。近似后验更新足以实现增益，交叉熵训练可用多项式样本复杂度恢复所需行为。

Conclusion: 上下文搜索的理论基础得到建立，其增益来源于反思对早期错误的定位能力，且该机制具有鲁棒性和可学习性，为扩展推理LLM的设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Training large language models (LLMs) with extended reasoning has enabled in-context search, in which models iteratively generate, critique, and revise solution attempts. We provide a theoretical analysis of in-context search by modeling it as approximate inference over reasoning traces, where the base model defines a prior and self-reflection provides feedback for posterior updates, and study the resulting inference-time sampling complexity - the number of sequential attempts needed to achieve high success probability. We show that when reflections reliably localize early mistakes, in-context search can yield exponential improvements over the base model, solving problems with exponentially small zero-shot pass rates using only a polynomial number of sequential attempts, whereas when this property fails, conditioning on past attempts offers no asymptotic benefit over parallel sampling. We further show that these gains are robust and learnable: approximate posterior updates suffice, and cross-entropy training on search rollouts recovers the required behavior with polynomial sample complexity. Finally, we show that under a stagewise abstraction of reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards, the optimal policy extension implements the same posterior reweighting rule. We validate key qualitative predictions of the theory on real large reasoning models.

</details>


### [129] [LLM-powered reasoning in agent-based modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06757)
*Sifat Afroj Moon,Dakotah Maguire,Adam Spannaus,Joe Tuccillo,Maksudul Alam,Sudip K. Seal,John Gounley,Heidi Hanson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出HALE框架，将大语言模型（LLMs）与基于智能体的建模（ABM）混合，利用LLMs预测人类决策行为以替代传统静态先验，使ABM能够实时适应变化，并在COVID-19模拟中验证可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ABM依赖静态先验，无法适应实时变化，存在信息缺口；而LLMs为预测人类决策提供了新机会，因此需要一种将LLMs与ABM结合的方法来弥补这一不足。

Method: 提出HALE（Hybrid Agent-based and Language-driven Epidemic）框架，在ABM模拟中引入LLMs来预测人类决策行为，以Salt Lake County的COVID-19模拟作为概念验证。

Result: 成功将LLMs集成到ABM中，实现了对人类决策的预测，并在COVID-19模拟中进行了概念验证。具体定量结果未在摘要中提及。

Conclusion: HALE框架有效解决了传统ABM依赖静态先验的问题，通过LLMs增强模型对实时变化的适应能力，为政策制定提供了更动态的建模工具。

Abstract: Agent-based modeling (ABM) has the capability to model millions of individuals and their interactions, which is useful for policy making. However, ABMs have traditionally relied on static prior, which prevents the models from adapting to real-time changes. Our research provides a novel approach to addressing this information gap. Large language models (LLMs) offer new opportunities to predict human decision-making. Here, we introduce a scalable Hybrid Agent-based and Language-driven Epidemic (HALE) modeling framework that leverages LLMs to predict human decision-making in an ABM simulation. As a proof-of-concept, we use HALE to simulate COVID-19 and its effects in Salt Lake County, UT.

</details>


### [130] [QANTIS: Hardware-Calibrated Sequential POMDP Belief Updates on IBM Heron](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06760)
*Bayram Yuksel Eker,Suayb S. Arslan,Ozgur Nazli,Mustafa Serhat Demirgil,Furkan Deligoz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: QANTIS 将量子处理器作为校准置信更新服务，在部分可观测 Tiger POMDP 场景中，通过多种 Grover 放大策略（无放大、有防护放大、全步固定点放大）验证了在 IBM Heron 硬件上跨步骤复用该服务时，返回的后验概率不会改变经典规划器的下游动作决策。全步 FPAA 在 8 步和 12 步主运行中保持了 Tiger 后验一致性，20 步和 32 步控制实验仍在同一运行区间内，所有硬件后验与精确贝叶斯后验在决策检查中选择相同动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在部分可观测自主系统中，能否将量子处理器作为校准置信更新服务跨步骤复用，而不破坏经典规划器所需的后验概率分布，从而验证量子计算在增强经典推理循环中的可行性。

Method: 采用 Tiger POMDP 作为基准问题，在 IBM Heron 量子硬件上进行受控案例研究。比较三种策略：无放大、有防护 Grover 放大、全步固定点放大（FPAA）。通过边界感知 BIQAE 稳定近零和近一的振幅估计，并使用罕见事件扫描映射百万分之一证据的样本复杂度包络。

Result: 全步 FPAA 在 8 步和 12 步主运行中保持了 Tiger 后验一致性，20 步和 32 步控制实验仍在同一运行区间内。所有决策检查点上，硬件后验与精确贝叶斯后验选择相同的即时动作。BIQAE 有效稳定了边界附近的振幅估计。

Conclusion: QANTIS 建立了一个硬件校准的置信更新原语的操作包络，而不是提出独立的硬件优势声明。量子处理器可以在特定条件下作为可信的后验更新服务嵌入经典规划循环。

Abstract: Autonomous systems under partial observability act on beliefs, not raw sensor events. QANTIS treats the quantum processor as a calibrated belief-update service in that loop: it receives a prior and an observation model, estimates the rare-event evidence term, and returns an ordinary posterior to a classical planner. This paper asks whether that service can be reused across a sequential Tiger POMDP horizon on present IBM Heron hardware without corrupting the planner-facing posterior. We answer with a controlled hardware case study rather than an end-to-end autonomy or wall-clock speedup claim. The study compares no amplification, guarded Grover amplification, and all-step fixed-point amplification on the same trajectory, then checks whether the returned posterior would change the downstream action. All-step FPAA preserves the Tiger posterior across the reported 8-step and 12-step primary runs, and the 20-step and 32-step controls remain inside the same operating band. In every reported decision check, the hardware posterior and the exact Bayes posterior select the same immediate action. Boundary-aware BIQAE stabilizes amplitude estimation near zero and near one, while a rare-event sweep maps the logical sample-complexity envelope for one-in-a-million evidence. The result is an operating envelope for a hardware-calibrated belief-update primitive, not a standalone hardware-advantage claim.

</details>


### [131] [Cost-Effective Agent Harnesses for Abstract Reasoning and Generalization on ARC-AGI-1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06764)
*Kabir Moghe,Peter Chin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究无需ARC专用微调或大量测试时计算的第三范式：使用开源模型DeepSeek V3.2（非思考模式），通过架构设计构建智能体管线，在严格预算下达到67.25% pass@2（每任务$0.62），将15.50%的基线提升约52个百分点。核心是分离模式发现与程序合成的Explorer-Definer Pipeline，以及具备自适应重新探索能力的Reflective Orchestrator。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索无需基准专用训练或大量测试时计算的开源模型在ARC-AGI-1上的性能上限，研究仅通过架构设计能恢复多少推理能力。

Method: (1) Explorer-Definer Pipeline：两阶段智能体管线，分离模式发现与可执行变换合成；(2) Reflective Orchestrator：增强管线，在先前假设在训练对上失败时自主探索新变换。使用DeepSeek V3.2非思考模式，无ARC微调。

Result: (1) Explorer-Definer Pipeline在ARC-AGI-1公开400任务集上达到57.50% pass@2，每任务$0.25；(2) Reflective Orchestrator达到67.25% pass@2，每任务$0.62；(3) 相比15.50%的one-shot基线提升约52个百分点；(4) pass@k分析表明管线受限于生成而非选择（训练对精度捕获约95%的候选上限）；(5) think工具移除导致pass@2下降5.75个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过架构驱动的智能体设计，无需专用微调或大量测试时计算即可显著提升ARC性能。模型受限于生成能力而非选择能力，改进方向应是更广泛的生成探索而非更好的排序。Reflective Orchestrator通过自适应重新探索验证了这一诊断。

Abstract: Recent progress on ARC-AGI-1 from disclosed architectures has come broadly from two regimes: heavy test-time compute over frontier models (evolutionary search, exhaustive sampling, extended chain-of-thought), or benchmark-specific training in which small models are fine-tuned on ARC data, often with task-specialized architectures. We study a third regime: an open-weight model in non-thinking mode (DeepSeek V3.2) under a strict budget, with no ARC-specific fine-tuning. We study what is recoverable through architecture alone, building agentic harnesses that decompose pattern-discovery and program-synthesis stages explicitly. First, we introduce an Explorer-Definer Pipeline that separates pattern discovery from executable transformation synthesis, implemented as a two-stage agent pipeline. Next, we present the Reflective Orchestrator, which augments the pipeline with autonomous exploration of new transformations when previous hypotheses fail on training pairs. On the ARC-AGI-1 public 400-task evaluation set, the pipeline reaches 57.50% pass@2 at \$0.25 per task, and the orchestrator reaches 67.25% pass@2 at \$0.62 per task. Together these architectures lift a 15.50% one-shot baseline by ~52 points without benchmark-specific training or heavy test-time compute. Furthermore, the orchestrator-driven lift tests a falsifiable diagnostic the pipeline produces; unbiased pass@k analysis suggests the pipeline is generation-bound, not selection-bound (selection via training-pair accuracy captures ~95% of the candidate ceiling) and predicts that significant improvement requires broader generation, not better ranking. The orchestrator implements this prediction via adaptive re-exploration and confirms it (unbiased pass@1 lift +9.81 pp, matching selection-mediated pass@2 lift). An additional pipeline ablation identifies its think tool as a significant component, with removal reducing pass@2 by 5.75 pp.

</details>


### [132] [Evaluating SageMath-Augmented LLM Agents for Computational and Experimental Mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06820)
*Pavel Snopov,German Magai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合LLM推理与SageMath计算机代数系统反馈的ReAct风格智能体框架，在RealMath基准上评估前沿模型，平均性能提升9.7个百分点，最高解决率达75.2%，表明CAS增强的智能体在辅助数学计算探索方面具有潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI for Math工作集中于自动形式化和定理证明，计算机代数系统(CAS)在LLM智能体工作流中的作用未被充分探索。

Method: 采用ReAct风格智能体框架，将LLM推理与SageMath的可验证反馈相结合，并使用Context7获取最新文档；同时改进了RealMath基准，引入多步后处理和多阶段验证流水线以提升问题集质量。

Result: 所有评估模型平均提升9.7个百分点（范围1.5~27.8pp），缩小了开源与闭源模型差距；Qwen 3.7-Max受益最大，GPT-5.5达到最高解决率75.2%且token使用量最低。

Conclusion: CAS增强的智能体是辅助数学计算探索的有前景方向，这项工作向着自动猜想发现迈出了一步。

Abstract: Recent advances in AI for Mathematics have focused largely on autoformalization and theorem proving, leaving the role of Computer Algebra Systems (CAS) in agentic LLM workflows underexplored. We propose a ReAct-style agentic setup that combines LLM reasoning with verifiable feedback from SageMath, together with Context7 for the up-to-date documentation. We evaluate this agentic setup across frontier models for solving research-level mathematical problems from the RealMath benchmark in a setting that emulates a computational-mathematics research loop. We also propose a refinement to the RealMath benchmark by introducing a multi-step post-processing procedure and a multi-stage validation pipeline, both of which improve the quality and reliability of the extracted problem set. Our experiments reveal substantial performance gains from SageMath access across all evaluated models on +9.7~pp on average, the gains range from 1.5~pp to 27.8~pp and narrow the gap between open-weight and closed models. Qwen~3.7-Max benefits from SageMath the most, while GPT-5.5 achieves the highest solve rate of $75.2\%$ and the lowest token usage among tool-enabled configurations. Our findings suggest that CAS-augmented agents represent a promising direction for assisting mathematicians in computational exploration, and we believe that this work is a step towards automated conjecture discovery. The project repository is available online.

</details>


### [133] [The Harness Effect: How Orchestration Design Sets the Token Economics of Enterprise Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06906)
*Muayad Sayed Ali,Aliaksandra Novik,Anji Boddupally,Artem Yavorskyi,Chris Nickerson,Daniel Rica,Emily DuGranrut,Felix Leung,Garrett Prince,Grace Barnett,Heath Robinson,Hosain Al Ahmad,Jesse Resnick,Juan Carlos Farah,Jyothi Swaroop Meruga,Leonid Kuznetsov,Luke Gorham,Marie Schmoll,Michael Paciullo,Saumya Das,Sharath Sheripally,Tommy Griscom,Mykyta Osadchyi,Neha Mantri,Nick Westrum,Olivia Benowitz,Parikshith Kulkarni,Radik Chernyshov,Rakshith Vasudev,Rohith Nadimpally,Vikas Gangadevi,Waseem AlShikh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现AI Agent开发中存在"Token最大化"现象（用更多token换取能力提升，但token消耗增速远超任务价值），并提出关键解决方案在于"Harness"（编排层）。通过控制变量实验（22个任务、6个模型），仅改变编排层即可使成本降低41%、时间缩短44%、token减少38%，同时质量持平或略有提升。这种效率提升与模型无关（所有模型成本降低33-61%），质量提升则与模型基础能力高度相关（r=0.99）。编排层对单位任务成本的影响甚至超过模型选择本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前Agentic AI开发存在"Token最大化"问题：通过延长推理链、增加交互轮次、扩大工具负载等方式用token换取能力，但token消耗增长速度超过任务价值增长速度。虽然token单价持续下降，但总支出仍在上升。作者认为解决这一问题的关键杠杆在于编排层（harness），而非模型本身。

Method: 采用控制变量实验设计：22个固定的评估任务、6个基础模型（Claude Sonnet 4.6, Gemini 3.1, Gemini Flash 3.5, Qwen 3.6, GLM 5.1, Palmyra X6），唯一变量为编排层——传统的生产循环 vs Writer Agent Harness。测量成本、耗时、token消耗、任务完成质量等指标，并分析效率提升与模型能力的关系。

Result: Harness编排层使混合成本降低41%（$0.21→$0.12），中位耗时减少44%（48s→27s），每个任务token消耗减少38%（14.2k→8.8k），任务完成质量持平（0.78→0.81，方向性差异）。所有模型成本均下降33-61%，质量提升与模型基础能力几乎完全相关（r=0.99）。质量/美元提升82%，每百万token完成任务数从54.9增至92.0。编排层对成本的影响超过模型菜单的完整跨度。

Conclusion: 编排层（harness）是应对Token最大化的决定性杠杆，其效率提升跨模型通用（无论当前还是未来模型）。本文形式化了编排层的token经济学（含提示缓存下的有效输入价格），详述了六大机制（从缓存形态约束到失败支出治理），并比较了六种主流Agent系统。核心论点：编排层是组织中唯一能将其效率倍增到每个运行模型上的组件。

Abstract: Agentic AI development today runs on token maxing: buying capability with tokens -- longer reasoning traces, more turns, wider tool payloads, bigger replayed contexts -- so tokens per task grow faster than task value. Falling per-token prices mask the pattern; total spend rises anyway. We argue the decisive lever against token maxing is the harness: the orchestration layer that assembles context, exposes tools, sequences turns, delegates work, and carries enterprise observability and governance. We isolate it with a controlled swap: 22 locked evaluation tasks, six foundation models (Claude Sonnet 4.6, Gemini 3.1, Gemini Flash 3.5, Qwen 3.6, GLM 5.1, Palmyra X6), changing only the orchestration layer -- a frozen conventional production loop versus the Writer Agent Harness. Holding models constant, the harness cuts blended cost per task 41% ($0.21->$0.12), median wall-clock 44% (48s->27s), and tokens per task 38% (14.2k->8.8k), with task-completion quality at parity (0.78->0.81, directional at this sample size). Efficiency is model-invariant -- every model gets cheaper (33-61%) -- while quality gains are capability-dependent: a model's gain correlates almost perfectly with its baseline strength (r=0.99, n=6), a phenomenon we term harness leverage. Quality per dollar rises 82%; task-completions per million tokens rise from 54.9 to 92.0. On this workload the orchestration layer moved cost per task more than the full spread of the model menu did. We formalize token economics at the orchestration layer (including effective input price under prompt caching), detail the six mechanism families behind the effect -- cache-shape discipline to failure-spend governance -- compare six widely used agent systems on the same axes, and argue the harness is the one component whose efficiency multiplies across every model an organization runs -- present and future.

</details>


### [134] [Grounding Spatial Relations in a Compact World Model: Instruction Leakage and a Goal-Free Dynamics Fix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06925)
*Yufeng Wang,Lu Wei,Haibin Ling*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一个关键问题：在目标条件化的世界模型中，当指令直接命名了待评分的量（如关系中的答案）时，模型会通过"指令转录"而非真正的感知来达到高准确率。作者通过实验证明这种"指令泄漏"现象，并提出了诊断方法和修复方案——将目标从动力学中移除，监督读取路径，从而恢复真正的、与指令无关的感知基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究目标条件化的紧凑世界模型在语言目标 grounding 中是否真正实现了感知，还是仅仅通过指令转录来欺骗性地达到高准确率。

Method: 通过三类实验检测指令泄漏：(1) 移除目标指令观察预测准确率下降（0.90→0.27）；(2) 使用反事实指令检验预测锚点是否跟随虚假指令（94.5%跟随假指令，仅2.3%跟随真实场景）；(3) 跨三种设置和内部消融实验验证。同时提出了修复方法：将目标从动力学中移除并监督读取路径。

Result: 目标条件化预测器达到0.90的关系读取准确率，但这是指令转录而非感知；移除目标后准确率跌至0.27；反事实指令下预测锚点94.5%跟随虚假指令。诊断发现：当指令命名了被评分量时，泄漏发生。修复后恢复了真正的、与指令无关的 grounding（0.88，有/无目标完全一致）。

Conclusion: 指令泄漏是目标条件化世界模型中的系统性陷阱，其特征是"被评分量可从指令中转录"且与模型对非指令输入的预测能力无关。诊断方法自然引出修复方案：保持目标在规划器的代价函数中而非动力学中，并监督读取路径。该检测协议和修复方法适用于任何指令命名了被评分量的目标条件化世界模型。

Abstract: Compact world models that condition on a language goal promise to ground relations such as ``put the red block left of the blue block'' using a sparse set of explicit \emph{reference anchors}. We ask when such references actually ground a relation, and identify a trap: a goal-conditioned predictor reaches a striking $0.90$ relation-readout accuracy, yet this is \emph{instruction transcription}, not perception. Withholding the goal collapses it to chance ($0.90\!\to\!0.27$, three seeds) and a counterfactual instruction makes the predicted anchors follow the \emph{false} instruction $94.5\%$ of the time (true scene $2.3\%$; $N{=}256$). Tested across three settings and a within-task ablation, our central claim characterizes the confound: \textbf{instruction leakage occurs when the scored quantity is transcribable from the instruction (when the instruction names the answer) and is essentially independent of how predictive the non-instruction inputs are.} Our tabletop and the external BabyAI benchmark leak, whereas a Language-Table forward-dynamics world model whose instruction names \emph{referents} does not, until the instruction is augmented to name the direction; and degrading the action never increases leakage, the opposite of what predictor-competition predicts. The diagnosis prescribes the fix: keep the goal out of the dynamics (it belongs to the planner's cost) and supervise the \emph{read} path, recovering genuine, instruction-independent grounding ($0.88$, identical with and without the goal). The detection protocol and remedy apply to any goal-conditioned world model whose instruction names the scored quantity.

</details>


### [135] [Large Behavior Model: A Promptable Digital Twin of the Retail Customer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06993)
*Wachiravit Modecrua,Krittin Pachtrachai,Touchapon Kraisingkorn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出大型行为模型(LBM)，通过统一的人物-环境框架从大规模零售交易中学习客户决策，在购买预测、负样本区分、购物篮完成、促销响应和跨域券兑换等任务上持续超越前沿通用语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么优化预测精度但缺乏可解释性，要么模拟用户但缺乏真实行为数据支撑，亟需一种既能准确建模又能基于真实行为数据提供解释的客户行为建模方法。

Method: 采用统一的人物-环境(PE)公式，用行为画像表示客户状态，通过检索增强生成(RAG)融入产品上下文；训练采用三阶段策略：行为数据语言化后的继续预训练、决策生成的监督微调、以及基于可验证奖励的强化学习进行证据校准。

Result: 在域内零售任务上一致超越前沿通用语言模型；在跨零售商和跨决策领域的零样本和微调迁移中表现强劲；消融实验表明继续预训练是行为泛化的主要驱动力，训练和推理阶段同时使用检索效果最佳，强化学习提高了对显式行为证据的依赖。

Conclusion: 交易历史中编码的行为知识可以被语言模型有效学习，为构建客户数字孪生和行为仿真提供了可扩展的基础。

Abstract: Customer behavior modeling underpins recommendation, marketing, and decision support, yet existing approaches either optimize predictive accuracy without explaining decisions or simulate users without grounding them in real behavioral data. We present the Large Behavioral Model (LBM) that learns customer decision making directly from large-scale retail transactions through a unified Person-Environment formulation. Customer state is represented by a behavioral profile derived from historical purchases, while product context is incorporated through retrieval-augmented generation. The model is trained using continued pre-training on verbalized behavioral data, supervised fine-tuning for decision generation, and reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards for evidence-based calibration. We evaluate the proposed framework on purchase prediction, hard-negative discrimination, basket completion, promotion response, and cross-domain voucher redemption. The model consistently outperforms frontier general-purpose language models on in-domain retail tasks while demonstrating strong zero-shot and fine-tuned transfer across retailers and decision domains. Ablation studies show that continued pre-training is the primary driver of behavioral generalization, retrieval is most effective when applied during both training and inference, and reinforcement learning improves reliance on explicit behavioral evidence over generic language-model priors. These results demonstrate that behavioral knowledge encoded in transaction histories can be effectively learned by language models, providing a scalable foundation for customer digital twins and behavior simulation.

</details>


### [136] [Learning social norms enhances compatibility in dynamic human-AI coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07021)
*Yi Yang,Siyuan Liu,Xin Gao,Huamu Sun,Chao Liu,Qing Zhou,Bingbing Nie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对AI代理在与人类动态交互中协调不足的问题，提出将隐性社会规范形式化为显式可量化原则（结果可预测性、价值对齐、优势意识），并在行人-车辆交互场景中验证。融入这些原则的LLM在闭环交互任务中总分比基线策略提升近4倍，比人-人交互高43%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI模型（包括LLM）在与人类进行动态交互时，往往难以以有效、体贴且自然的方式协调。作者假设这一差距源于现有方法仅将模型行为与人类演示对齐，而未明确量化生成这些行为的底层社会规范。

Method: 选取行人-车辆交互作为代表性动态交互场景，开发简化实验平台收集3,456次动态人类交互数据，从中识别出三项社会规范原则（结果可预测性、价值对齐、优势意识），并将其融入AI代理的决策过程。

Result: 融入社会规范的LLM在与人闭环交互任务中，总分比基线策略提升近4倍，且比人-人交互表现高43%。

Conclusion: 将隐性社会规范形式化为显式可量化原则，能够使AI代理在动态交互中实现互利协调，从而支持其更自然地融入人类社会。

Abstract: Humans continuously coordinate with others in dynamic interactions, often through implicit, hard-to-quantify social norms that act as shared tacit expectations among interacting agents. As AI agents, including large language models (LLMs), become embedded in daily life, they increasingly participate in such interactions and reshape social interaction structures. Yet they often fail to coordinate with humans in an effective, considerate, and natural manner. We hypothesize that this gap arises because existing approaches align model behavior with human demonstrations without explicitly quantifying the underlying norms that generate such behavior. We selected pedestrian-vehicle interaction as a representative dynamic interaction and developed a simplified experimental platform that captures its key interactive features. From 3,456 dynamic human interactions collected via this platform, we identified three principles underlying human social norms: outcome predictability, value alignment, and advantage awareness. Incorporating these principles into AI agents significantly improves human-AI coordination. In the closed-loop interaction task with humans, the social-norm-informed LLM achieved a nearly fourfold higher total score than the baseline strategy and outperformed human-human interactions by 43%. These findings indicate that formalizing tacit social norms into explicit, quantifiable principles can enable AI agents to achieve mutually beneficial coordination in dynamic interactions, supporting their more natural integration into human society.

</details>


### [137] [Measuring Intelligence Beyond Human Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07040)
*Jerry Han,Rafael Moschopoulos,Ella Colby,Vishrut Goyal,Andrew Tu,Kia Ghods,Mark Braverman,Elad Hazan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于相对测量的对抗性心理测量评级系统，通过让模型生成公开挑战来区分不同系统，从而解决超越人类能力的智能评估问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试在达到人类水平后会出现饱和，且超越人类能力时，评估者难以确定哪些任务是困难且可验证的。作者认为这种困难是绝对尺度评估固有的，因此需要新的评估范式。

Method: 提出相对测量范式：模型生成公开挑战来区分其他系统，聚合这些结果形成对抗性心理测量评级系统。设计了减少私人信息攻击激励的实用协议，支持无评判者裁决，并自然随系统能力扩展。

Result: 该框架在可验证和开放式的非可验证领域均得到实例化，证明了模型生成的评估可以持续衡量超越人类前沿的系统。

Conclusion: 超越人类能力的智能测量需要从绝对尺度转向相对尺度的对抗性评估范式，模型自身生成的挑战可以成为持续评估的工具。

Abstract: How can we measure intelligence beyond human capability?
  Human-authored benchmarks saturate, and above human capability, examiners may not know which tasks are both hard and verifiable. We argue that this difficulty is inherent to absolute-scale evaluation and propose a new paradigm based on relative measurement in which models generate public challenges that separate other systems. Aggregating these outcomes yields an adversarial psychometric rating system that can scale with the systems being measured. We describe practical protocols that reduce incentives for private-information attacks, support judge-free adjudication, and naturally scale with agent capabilities. We instantiate the framework across verifiable and open-ended, non-verifiable domains, illustrating how model-generated evaluation can continue to measure systems beyond the human frontier.

</details>


### [138] [Operational Reframing and Approval-Framed Delegation in Multi-Agent LLM Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07097)
*Lifei Liu,Haoran Yu,Xiaochong Jiang,Su Wang,Pin Qian,Yihang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文批判了多智能体LLM系统安全评估中常用的“流水线效应”聚合指标，提出其混淆了三种机制（操作重构、规划器行为、委托框架）。通过五条件对比实验设计，在30个合成场景和四个基准数据集上发现：聚合安全并非稳定的架构属性；操作重构是最具迁移性的风险信号；原始模型排名无法预测部署后的规划器-执行器行为。建议安全评估应分别报告各机制而非笼统归因于架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常将直接提示与规划器-执行器流水线之间的安全差异归为一个聚合的“流水线效应”，但这一指标难以解释，因为它混合了三种不同机制：恶意意图被重构为合理操作、规划器拒绝或转换请求、执行器在委托提示下行事。需要分离这些因素以准确理解多智能体系统的安全风险。

Method: 提出五条件受控对比设计，在30个合成有害场景和四个智能体安全基准（使用LLM判断合规性）上进行评估。通过不同条件分离操作重构、规划器行为、委托框架三种机制的影响。

Result: 1) 聚合流水线安全不是稳定的架构属性；2) 操作重构是最具迁移性的风险信号，GPT、Gemini、DeepSeek均受影响，Claude相对抵抗；3) 规划器主要通过拒绝来抵消风险，但产生可执行步骤时，执行器合规性可能超过直接操作基线；4) 委托提示对提示设计、模型配对和场景来源敏感；5) 原始直接提示下的模型排名无法预测部署后行为，如Gemini在原始评估中最安全但与Claude规划器配对后合规率从8.9%升至38.9%。

Conclusion: 多智能体安全评估应分别报告操作重构、规划器行为、委托框架和模型配对的影响，而非将失败笼统归因于架构本身。

Abstract: Safety evaluations of multi-agent LLM systems often compare a direct prompt with a planner-executor pipeline and report the difference as a single "pipeline effect." We argue that this aggregate is difficult to interpret because it conflates three mechanisms: harmful intent may be reframed as plausible operational work, the planner may refuse or transform the request, and the executor may act under delegation prompts implying prior approval. To separate these factors, we introduce a five-condition controlled contrast design, evaluated on 30 synthetic harmful scenarios and an exploratory external validation set from four agent-safety benchmarks using LLM-judged compliance.
  Our results show that aggregate pipeline safety is not a stable architectural property. Operational reframing is the most portable risk signal, increasing compliance for GPT, Gemini, and DeepSeek across both scenario sets, while Claude is comparatively resistant. Planner behavior can offset this risk mainly through refusal; however, when the planner produces executable steps, the executor may become more compliant than under the direct operational baseline. Approval-framed delegation is sensitive to prompt design, model pairing, and scenario source, and a skeptical executor prompt sharply reduces compliance.
  Raw-direct model rankings can also mispredict deployed planner-executor behavior. Gemini is safest under raw direct prompts in the primary set yet shows the largest amplification with a Claude planner, rising from 8.9 percent to 38.9 percent compliance. GPTs near-zero aggregate pipeline effect instead hides a reframing increase canceled by planner refusal. These findings suggest that multi-agent safety evaluations should report reframing, planner behavior, delegation framing, and model pairing separately before attributing failures to architecture itself.

</details>


### [139] [Does AI Understand Imaging? A Systematic Benchmark of Agentic AI for Computational Imaging Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07189)
*Ethan Chung,Chuanjun Zheng,Jasper Tan,Jingxi Li,Haopeng Zhang,Huaijin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ImagingBench，一个包含20项计算成像任务的基准测试，涵盖射线/波动光学、图像信号处理、逆重建、计算感知和校准五大类别。通过专家引导、规划器引导和前向仿真三种评估设置，测试了主流视觉-语言模型（如Gemini、GPT、Qwen）在计算成像任务上的表现。结果表明，尽管这些模型能生成视觉上合理的输出，但其物理保真度远逊于专用方法，尤其在无透镜成像、事件重建、飞行时间成像和全息术等计算感知任务上表现薄弱，揭示了语义视觉能力与物理成像能力之间的显著鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言模型和智能体AI在语义视觉任务上表现优异，但能否处理计算成像中的物理和逆问题尚不清楚。为了系统评估这一差距，作者构建了ImagingBench基准测试。

Method: 设计20个计算成像任务，分为五类（射线/波动光学、图像信号处理、逆重建、计算感知、校准）。设置三种评估模式：Expert（固定专家引导逆重建）、Planner（规划器引导逆重建）和Forward（前向仿真一致性检查）。对Gemini、GPT、Qwen等主流多模态模型进行基准测试，并与任务专用非智能体基线方法对比。

Result: 智能体模型在所有任务上的表现均弱于专用方法，尤其在计算感知问题（无透镜成像、事件重建、飞行时间成像、全息术）上差距显著。Planner引导相比固定提示的Expert基线仅有微小且不一致的提升。模型虽能生成视觉上合理的输出，但参考保真度指标较差。

Conclusion: 语义视觉能力与物理基础成像性能之间存在实质性鸿沟，ImagingBench为衡量这一差距和追踪智能体AI在计算成像领域的进展提供了统一测试平台。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) and agentic AI have shown strong performance on semantic visual tasks, but it remains unclear whether they can handle the physics and inverse problems that underlie computational imaging. We present ImagingBench, a benchmark of 20 computational imaging tasks spanning five categories: ray and wave optics, image signal processing, inverse reconstruction, computational sensing, and calibration. ImagingBench evaluates three complementary settings: Expert, fixed expert-guided inverse reconstruction; Planner, planner-guided inverse reconstruction; and Forward, forward-system simulation for consistency checking. We benchmark leading proprietary and open-source image-centric multimodal systems, including Gemini, GPT, and Qwen, and compare them with representative task-specific non-agentic baselines. Across tasks, agentic models remain consistently weaker than specialized methods, especially on computational sensing problems such as lensless imaging, event-based reconstruction, time-of-flight imaging, and holography. Planner guidance provides only modest and inconsistent gains over the fixed-prompt Expert baseline.
